//! Writes: the four words, their rules, the constraints (unique, cap,
//! immutable, append-only, after-insert), maintained aggregates, and the
//! partition events every row change produces.

use super::runner::Runner;
use super::validate::validate_row;
use crate::error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError};
use crate::partition;
use crate::rows::index_values;
use crate::rows::{Bounds, Dir};
use crate::schema::{Constraint, Effect, Table, materialize_optional_fields};
use crate::value::{Supplied, Value, canonical_json};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Origin {
    Source,
    Maintained,
    LiveAuthority,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub(super) struct FinalExistsWitness {
    table: String,
    row: Value,
    constraint: usize,
}

#[path = "write_references.rs"]
mod references;
#[path = "write_target.rs"]
pub(crate) mod target;

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn insert(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.insert_mode(table, row, false).map(|_| ())
    }

    pub(super) fn insert_if_absent(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.insert_mode(table, row, true)
    }

    fn insert_mode(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
            ));
        }
        if self.device && if_absent && self.schema.tables[table_name].through.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("insert if absent on a read-through child queues without a row".into()));
        }
        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
            let mark = runner.meter.mark();
            let result = runner.insert_attempt(table_name, row, if_absent, None).map_err(|error| error.authorization_context(table_name, Effect::Insert));
            // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — denied direct allocations hide deferred
            // read witnesses and counter work just as NOT_FOUND hides targets.
            if matches!(&result, Err(RuntimeError::Rule { effect: Effect::Insert, .. } | RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { .. }))
                && runner.schema.tables.get(table_name).is_some_and(|table| !table.sequences.is_empty()) {
                runner.meter.restore(mark);
            }
            result
        })
    }

    fn insert_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool, upsert_index: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Insert)?;
        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, &row, true)?;
        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, &row)?;
        let supplied_sequence = super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, &row);
        // A directly supplied field is a payload error before any lookup;
        // only an upsert's copied address waits for insert authority.
        if upsert_index.is_none() && supplied_sequence.is_err() { return supplied_sequence.map(|_| false); }
        let supplied = row.clone();
        let row = self.with_defaults(table_name, row)?;
        if self.schema.tables[table_name].ephemeral.is_some() { self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?; }
        let row = row.ephemeral(&self.schema.tables[table_name], self.context.now.unwrap_or(0))?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — reserve, then stamp the parent capture before any rule sees the image.
        self.reserve_capture(&self.schema.tables[table_name])?;
        let row = self.capture_insert(table_name, row)?;
        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        let independent = !table.sequences.is_empty() && !super::rules::deciding_fields(table_name, table, &table.rules.insert)
            .iter().any(|field| table.sequences.contains_key(field));
        // An independent denial must never consult a hidden read witness.
        let authorized = independent && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)?;
        if independent && !authorized {
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
        }
        if !table.sequences.is_empty() {
            // Admission reserves the worst case before either read verdict;
            // restoring a refusal alone cannot hide a boundary abort.
            // An independent insert rule was already prepaid and evaluated.
            // Only still-pending checks belong in this preflight allowance.
            let insert_checks = if authorized { 0 } else { 1 + u64::from(upsert_index.is_some() && supplied_sequence.is_ok()) };
            self.reserve_sequence_authorization(table, &row, insert_checks)?;
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — an unreadable allocation cannot expose counter,
        // uniqueness or cap state, even to a principal allowed to insert.
        if !self.sequence_insert_readable(table_name, &row)? {
            self.refusing_clause(table_name, "sequence read authority");
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "sequence read authority") });
        }
        if let Err(error) = supplied_sequence {
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — absent upserts decide insert authority before field diagnostics.
            // A target key may supply an ordinal even when the patch does not.
            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
                return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
            }
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
        }
        if upsert_index.is_some() {
            self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied).map_err(|_| self.no_write_target())?;
        } else { self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied)?; }
        // The image copy an event slot costs is the writer's own supplied bytes (LLP 3000.015 §1).
        let image_bytes = row.heap_bytes();
        let (row, sequence_full) = self.sequence_fields(table_name, row)?;
        let row = self.receipt_insert_fields(table_name, row)?;
        if let Some(index) = upsert_index {
            if self.device { self.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row)?; }
            // Preserve upsert's target preflight before the ordinary insert checks;
            // only this first authority refusal is an undisclosed target miss.
            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? { return Err(self.no_write_target()); }
            if sequence_full { return Err(super::sequences::exhausted()); }
            // Engine event slots are stamped after predecessor/sequence checks,
            // just as on an ordinary insert; preflight validates authored fields.
            if let Err(error) = super::validate::validate_before_slots(self.schema, table_name, &row) {
                return Err(if matches!(error, RuntimeError::Input(_)) && !self.context.is_system() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
            }
        }
        if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
            return Err(self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule"));
        }
        // Retry keys independent of engine allocation still identify a no-op
        // at the ceiling. Never use the clamped authority candidate as a key.
        if sequence_full && if_absent {
            let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| {
                index.unique && index.columns().iter().all(|column| !table.sequences.contains_key(column))
            }) {
                let key = index_values(index, &row);
                if key.contains(&Value::Null) { continue; }
                if self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, None, Effect::Insert)?.is_some() {
                    self.observe_sequence_columns(table_name, &row, &index.columns(), true)?;
                    return Ok(false);
                }
            }
        }
 M WORKSTREAM-REPORT.md

//! Birth and mirroring of maintained fields. Private totals are authoritative.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — never probe parent authority or resurrect expired rows.
use super::runner::Runner;
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
use super::write::Origin;
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, schema::{Maintain, reservations::{COLUMNS, ASSETS}}, value::Value};
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
use crate::schema::maintained_id;

pub(crate) fn write_result(table: &crate::schema::Table, mut row: Value) -> Value {
    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.retain(|field,_| !table.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
    row
}

/// Preparation replaces contribution indexes, so refuse forbidden dependencies
/// while the caller's resolved declarations are still present.
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
pub(super) fn validate_before_prepare(schema: &crate::schema::Schema) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::columns::timeline_issues(schema).first() {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN", message: issue.message.clone() });
    }
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::columns::validate(schema).first() {
        let mut message=format!("{}: {}",issue.path,issue.message);
        for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.is_some()) { message=message.replace(&m.target,&m.label()); }
        return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(message));
    }
    Ok(())
}


impl Runner<'_> {
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn rebuild_imported_columns(&mut self) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
        let declarations: Vec<_> = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.is_some()).cloned().collect();
        for m in declarations {
            self.rebuild_maintain(&m)?;
            self.mirror_column_pass(&m)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — devices preserve synchronized column values;
    // only the server reads private totals or performs their mirrored writes.
    #[allow(unused_mut)] // The server fills the private values; the device only charges.
    pub(super) fn birth_columns(&mut self, name: &str, mut row: Value) -> Result<Value,RuntimeError> {
        let price = self.source_price(name, crate::schema::reservations::BIRTH)?;
        self.reservation_deferred |= self.device && price[2] != 0;
        self.charge_price(price)?;
        if self.device { return Ok(row); }
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        {
        let declarations: Vec<_> = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|o.table == name)).cloned().collect();
        if declarations.is_empty() { return Ok(row); }
        let id = row.field("id")?.clone();
        for m in declarations {
            let total = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &m.target,&maintained_id(&[id.clone()])))?
                .and_then(|r|r.field("value").ok().cloned()).unwrap_or(Value::Int(0));
            if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.insert(m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column.clone(),total); }
        }
        }
        Ok(row)
    }

    // Without the server feature the interpreter only runs projected device
    // schemas. Those contain no private maintains, so there is nothing to mirror.
    #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
    pub(super) fn mirror_column(&mut self, _m: &Maintain, _key: &[Value], _total: i64) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> { Ok(()) }

    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn mirror_column(&mut self, m: &Maintain, key: &[Value], total: i64) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
        if self.column_rebuild { return Ok(()) }
        let Some(owner) = &m.private else { return Ok(()) };
        self.refuse_timeline_write(&owner.table, crate::schema::Effect::Update)?;
        let Some(Value::Id(id)) = key.first() else { return Ok(()) };
        self.written_tables.insert(owner.table.clone());
        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined,1)?;
        let table = self.schema.tables[&owner.table].clone();
        let Some(old) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &owner.table,id))? else { return Ok(()) };
        if table.expired(&old,self.context.now.unwrap_or(0)) { return Ok(()) }
        if old.field(&owner.column).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Int(0)) == Value::Int(total) { return Ok(()) }
        let mut row = old.clone();
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.insert(owner.column.clone(),Value::Int(total)); }
        let entries = self.tx.put(&owner.table,&table,&row,Some(&old))?;
        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries,entries)?;
        self.row_changed(&owner.table,Some(&old),Some(&row),Origin::Maintained)
    }

    // All source attempts use the same complete plan, independent of receipts.
    pub(super) fn reserve_columns(&mut self, source: &str) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
        // Preserve the write authority/schema refusal for an unknown raw target.
        if self.schema.table(source).is_none() { return Ok(()); }
        let price = self.source_price(source, COLUMNS)?;
        self.reservation_deferred |= self.device && price[3] != 0;
        self.charge_price(price)?;
        self.column_heap = price[4];
        self.column_prepaid = price[3] > 0;
        let assets = self.source_price(source, ASSETS)?;
        if self.device || self.tx.holds_assets() {
            self.charge_price(assets)?;
            self.column_heap = self.column_heap.saturating_add(assets[4]);
        }
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { self.assets_prepaid = true; }
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){
    report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());
    let rank=|c:&Counterexample|match c.reachability{Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace=>0,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace=>1,_=>2};
    let committed=|c:&Counterexample|c.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    for c in &campaign.violations {for v in &mut report.invariants {if v.invariant.site.to_string()!=c.invariant_site{continue;}
        if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|rank(old)<rank(c)||rank(old)==rank(c)&&committed(old)<=committed(c)){continue;}
        v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());
        v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; fixture origin is explicit, not an empty-state reachability theorem".into());
    }}
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->Result<usize,String>{
    // Auth admission is exercised on the actual schema. External worker/native
    // startup needs its app-owned artifacts; never pretend this schema-only
    // host qualifies those paths.
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.native||p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)
        || !bound.schema.effects.is_empty() || !bound.schema.action_specs.is_empty(){
        println!("security not covered: unauthenticated serve HTTP for native/action/effect applications needs its complete deployed artifacts");return Ok(0);
    }
    let stamp=SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_nanos();
    let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-security-serve-{}-{stamp}",std::process::id()));
    fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;let scratch=Scratch(root);
    fs::create_dir(scratch.0.join("snapback")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    for (name,text) in sources {
        let file=std::path::Path::new(name).file_name().ok_or("source needs a file name")?;
        fs::write(scratch.0.join("snapback").join(file),text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    }
    let executable=scratch.0.join("security-host");
    image::snapshot(&executable)?;
    let output=fs::File::create(scratch.0.join("host.log")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let command=|verb:&str|->Result<Command,String>{
        let mut c=Command::new(&executable);c.arg(verb).current_dir(&scratch.0)
            .env("SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID",std::process::id().to_string()).stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(output.try_clone().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).stderr(output.try_clone().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);Ok(c)
    };
    let mut deploy=Process(command("deploy")?.args(["deployment"]).spawn().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);
    wait(&mut deploy,Duration::from_secs(30))?;drop(deploy);
    let listener=TcpListener::bind((std::net::Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST,0)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let address=listener.local_addr().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;drop(listener);
    let mut host=Process(command("serve")?.args(["deployment","--bind","127.0.0.1","--port",&address.port().to_string()]).spawn().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);
    let until=Instant::now()+Duration::from_secs(15);
    loop{
        if host.0.try_wait().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_some(){return Err("private production serve exited during startup".into());}
        if TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address,Duration::from_millis(100)).is_ok(){break;}
        if Instant::now()>=until{return Err("private production serve did not listen within 15 seconds".into());}
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    }
    let mut attempted=0;
    for p in &bound.programs {
        let prefix=match p.program.kind{snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Query=>"q",snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation=>"m",_=>continue};
        for forged in [false,true]{
            let (status,body)=request(address,&format!("/{prefix}/{}",p.program.name),forged)?;attempted+=1;
            let ephemeral=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation&&bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral);
            if !auth_refusal(status,&body,ephemeral) {
                return Err(format!("unauthenticated serve HTTP {} (forged persona={forged}) returned {status}: {body}",p.program.name));
            }
        }
    }
    // Staging/provisioning belongs to STAGE. This host never opens its dev doors.
    for path in ["/seed","/dev/reset","/dev/clock/advance"] {
        let (status,_)=request(address,path,false)?;attempted+=1;
        if !matches!(status,401|403|404){return Err(format!("production development door {path} returned {status}"));}
    }
    let revoked=revoked_sessions(bound,address)?;
    drop(host);drop(scratch);
    println!("security unauthenticated serve HTTP: {attempted} attempts refused (including forged personas and dev doors)");
    Ok(attempted+revoked)
}
 for operator in [false,true]{
  let source=format!("table operators:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  insert <- {}\n  online only\ntable secrets:\n  read <- exists operators[viewer]\n  online only\ninvariant operators.insert only viewer = 'service:ops'\nmutation enroll():\n  return insert operators {{ principal: viewer }}\n",if operator{"viewer = 'service:ops'"}else{".principal = viewer"});
  let b=bound(&source);let mut r=security::analyze(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("policy.q".into(),source)],&b);assert_eq!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"),!operator,"{:?}",r.notes);
 }
}
#[test]
fn principal_only_current_membership_can_revoke_retained_grants(){
 let source="table operators:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  immutable principal\n  read <- allow\n  delete <- viewer = 'service:ops'\n  online only\ntable grants:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  insert <- exists operators[viewer]\n  online only\ntable records:\n  read <- exists grants[viewer]\n  online only\ninvariant records.read only exists operators[viewer]\nmutation revoke(who: principal):\n  delete operators[who]\n  return null\n";
 let r=security::analyze(&bound(source));assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE"&&n.message.starts_with("records.read")),"{:?}",r.notes);
}

Guard dependence includes aliases of inserted rows and subsequent reads of the
written table, including post-insert require/check rollback. An argument's
single textual occurrence does not establish independence when a guard observes
its stored value through such an alias; unresolved guard aliases stay uncovered.
Packed qualification requires an actual policy or isolated HTTP challenge,
records the two counts separately, and retains explicit native campaign gaps
(e.g. the existing unsupported ephemeral-hub fork) rather than claiming native
mutation coverage from HTTP probes.

Packed social qualification exposed a complete native campaign skip when an
otherwise durable app merely declared an ephemeral table. Fixture-only forks
now copy the hub under its lock into independent memory, preserving row/index
state, deadlines, per-principal/global quota accounting and limits, epoch, ring
and generation binding. The copy never retains the live publisher or external
presence. A mismatched hub/store generation refuses. These are quiescent test
fixture copies, not live adoption or wire changes (LLP3000.017 §§3–4).
Durable generated initialization and histories then run normally in mixed apps.
Generated ephemeral populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain explicit
coverage gaps; their mutation programs are skipped rather than dispatched
through the durable mutation door. External presence still refuses a fixture
fork. Packed social must exercise actual native challenges, not a zero-work pass.

The unsupported ephemeral-program boundary applies in every dispatch phase,
including column/row paired observers and foreign-asset challenges, with a
central observer refusal as a backstop. No phase credits a skipped ephemeral
mutation as a reachable witness. Durable query controls still execute.
Column independence follows aliases into other written fields/tables as well
as guards. A creator copying an inserted private value into a related marker
constrains the alternate world; changing only the source value cannot turn
an always-public id list into a column disclosure. Alias-dependent operators
and opaque effects likewise stay conservatively uncovered.

Alias dependence is field-sensitive across compiler-owned object projections:
a copied row id does not couple an independently supplied private payload.
Unknown, defaulted or implicit upsert field sources remain uncovered. An
immutable-column alternate that must rebuild the SQL fixture declines when
the original hub has committed ephemeral state; it must not reset that state
and attribute the resulting public-presence difference to a protected column.
Actual typed disclosures and state-preserving forks remain available.

**NOT READY** for source commit `6024498db7be03a753d77d2bd7166eca6448d54b`.

Two material false-positive blockers reproduced from an isolated build of that exact commit:

1. **Generated bootstrap bypasses owned-field authority.** [generated.rs:108](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/generated.rs:108) checks the table insert predicate, then calls system-authorized `seed_at`.

   Repro:
   ```quarry
   table docs:
     owner: principal
     mark: int default 0 written by .owner readable by audience
     read <- allow
     insert <- viewer = 'service:operator' and .owner != viewer
     online only
   invariant docs always .mark = 0
   ```
   Generation creates `mark=1` rows and credits two failing `GeneratedFixtureTrace` assertions. Ordinary insertion cannot supply that value: the admitted operator cannot own the field. Bootstrap must execute complete ordinary admission.

2. **Implicit upsert defaults escape column-coupling checks.** [column_reads.rs:120](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/column_reads.rs:120) handles the `Upsert` tag but misses compiler-emitted `WriteTarget { upsert: true }`.

   Repro:
   ```quarry
   table docs:
     principal: principal
     body: int default 0
     unique principal
     immutable body
     read <- allow
     insert <- allow
     online only
   mutation create():
     upsert docs[viewer] {}
     return null
   query publicIds():
     return docs where .body = 0 first 50 by .id {id}
   invariant docs.body read only deny
   ```
   The exact-commit repro credits four `GeneratedFixtureTrace` disclosures from `publicIds`, inventing an immutable `body=1` alternate. Section 13.7 requires this implicit/defaulted source to remain uncovered.

Focused controls passed: **21 language, 18 core ephemeral, 8 CLI**, including the real password-only mixed-ephemeral authentication regression. Those controls miss these two cases. I did not repeat the broad histories target.

This verdict concerns source readiness only. Separate cross-platform qualification remains separate; no publication readiness is claimed.
